  if (length of "Code"=="0"):
    CALL example()
  forever:
    if mousedown:

      CALL click()
      wait_until not(mousedown)
    wait ??4,0.02

PROC click  [warp=false] args=[]
  if (V(uy)>"126"):
    if ((V(ux)>"51") and (V(ux)<"116")):
      CALL startRun %s("0")
    if (not((V(ux)<"116")) and (V(ux)<"170")):
      CALL startRun %s("1")
    if (not((V(ux)<"170")) and (V(running)=="1")):
      stopreq = ??4,1
      running = ??4,0
      status = ??10,Stopped
      broadcast ??11,HALT,i,~TZiDFGzdK}G_O6OmY
      dirty = ??4,1
  if ((V(busy)=="0") and (V(running)=="0")):
    if (((V(uy)<"108") and (V(uy)>"-116")) and (V(ux)<"58")):
      selected = (V(page)+"floor"((("106"-V(uy))/"14")))
      if (V(selected)>length of "Code"):
        selected = length of "Code"
      dirty = ??4,1
      cursor = "hscroll"
      if ("ux">"-201"):
        cursor = ("hscroll"+"floor"((("ux"+"201")/"5.1")))
      if ("cursor">len(item "selected" of "Code")):
        cursor = len(item "selected" of "Code")
      if ("cursor"<"0"):
        cursor = ??4,0
      saved = ??4,0
      CALL keepCursor()
    if (((V(uy)<"-122") and (V(uy)>"-146")) and (V(ux)<"52")):
      if (V(ux)<"-185"):
        CALL importCode()
      if (not((V(ux)<"-183")) and (V(ux)<"-138")):
        if (V(exportShown)=="0"):
          CALL exportCode()
        else:
          hidelist Export
          exportShown = ??4,0
          status = ??10,Export list hidden
      if (not((V(ux)<"-136")) and (V(ux)<"-91")):
        CALL example()
      if (not((V(ux)<"-89")) and (V(ux)<"-44")):
        busy = ??4,1
        ask ??10,Clear program? Type YES to confirm
        if (answer=="YES"):
          CALL backup()
          clear(Code)
          append(Code, ??10,)
          selected = ??4,1
          page = ??4,1
          cursor = ??4,0
          hscroll = ??4,0
          saved = ??4,0
          status = ??10,New file
        busy = ??4,0
      if (not((V(ux)<"-42")) and (V(ux)<"3")):
        CALL undo()
      if (not((V(ux)<"5")) and (V(ux)<"51")):
        CALL help()
      dirty = ??4,1
    if (((V(uy)>"-118") and (V(uy)<"118")) and ((V(ux)>"-230") and (V(ux)<"-206"))):
      if ((timer-V(lastclick))<"0.5"):
        lastclick = ??4,-99
        CALL editLine()
      else:
        lastclick = timer
  if (((V(busy)=="0") and (V(running)=="0")) and (((V(ux)>"67") and (V(ux)<"231")) and ((V(uy)>"-154") and (V(uy)<"-122")))):
    ui = ("floor"(((V(ux)-"68")/"13.35"))+"1")
    if (V(ui)<"1"):
      ui = ??4,1
    if (V(ui)>"12"):
      ui = ??4,12
    upal = ??12,ui,`C50FyRqThpfY1ZIJHBl
    if (V(uy)>"-138"):
      if (V(upal)=="1"):
        CALL tab()
      if (V(upal)=="2"):
        CALL newline()
      if (V(upal)=="3"):
        CALL backspace()
      if (V(upal)=="4"):
        CALL insert %s(":")
      if (V(upal)=="5"):
        CALL insert %s("(")
      if (V(upal)=="6"):
        CALL insert %s(")")
      if (V(upal)=="7"):
        CALL insert %s("=")
      if (V(upal)=="8"):
        CALL insert %s(",")
      if (V(upal)=="9"):
        CALL insert %s("'")
      if (V(upal)=="10"):
        CALL insert %s("\"")
      if (V(upal)=="11"):
        CALL insert %s("+")
      if (V(upal)=="12"):
        CALL insert %s("-")
    else:
      if (V(upal)=="1"):
        CALL insert %s("*")
      if (V(upal)=="2"):

      if (V(upal)=="3"):

PROC startRun %s  [warp=false] args=["sm"]
  if (V(busy)=="0"):
    if (V(running)=="0"):
      mode = arg:"sm"
      ticket = ??4,1
      stopreq = ??4,0
      running = ??4,1
      broadcast ??11,RUN,!W=mh2F6%LN7_*e?PEzV
    else:
      mode = arg:"sm"
      ticket += ??4,1

WHEN event_whenkeypressed up arrow
  if (((mousex>"67") and (mousex<"230")) and ((mousey>"-118") and (mousey<"29"))):
    CALL scrollone %s %s("2", "-1")
  else:
    if (((mousex>"67") and (mousex<"230")) and ((mousey>"29") and (mousey<"118"))):
      CALL scrollone %s %s("1", "-1")
    else:
      if (((mousex>"-230") and (mousex<"-206")) and ((mousey>"-118") and (mousey<"118"))):
        CALL scrollone %s %s("3", "-1")
      else:
        if (V(busy)=="0"):
          selected += ??4,-1
          if (V(selected)<"1"):
            selected = ??4,1
          CALL reveal()

WHEN event_whenkeypressed down arrow
  if (((mousex>"67") and (mousex<"230")) and ((mousey>"-118") and (mousey<"29"))):
    CALL scrollone %s %s("2", "1")
  else:
    if (((mousex>"67") and (mousex<"230")) and ((mousey>"29") and (mousey<"118"))):
      CALL scrollone %s %s("1", "1")
    else:
      if (((mousex>"-230") and (mousex<"-206")) and ((mousey>"-118") and (mousey<"118"))):
        CALL scrollone %s %s("3", "1")
      else:
        if (V(busy)=="0"):
          selected += ??4,1
          if (V(selected)>length of "Code"):
            selected = length of "Code"
          CALL reveal()

WHEN event_whenkeypressed left arrow
  CALL moveCursor %s("-1")

WHEN event_whenkeypressed right arrow
  CALL moveCursor %s("1")

WHEN event_whenkeypressed space
  CALL insert %s(" ")

PROC reveal  [warp=true] args=[]
  if (V(selected)<V(page)):
    page = ??12,selected,PZ{E8r-M#W3WR*_R=Yg=
  if (V(selected)>(V(page)+"15")):
    page = (V(selected)-"15")
  dirty = ??4,1
  saved = ??4,0
  CALL keepCursor()

PROC backup  [warp=true] args=[]
  clear(Snapshot)
  ui = ??4,1
  repeat length of "Code":
    append(Snapshot, item V(ui) of "Code")
    ui += ??4,1

PROC undo  [warp=true] args=[]
  if (length of "Snapshot">"0"):
    clear(Code)
    ui = ??4,1
    repeat length of "Snapshot":
      append(Code, item V(ui) of "Snapshot")
      ui += ??4,1
    selected = ??4,1
    page = ??4,1
    status = ??10,Restored last edit

PROC editLine  [warp=false] args=[]
  busy = ??4,1
  ask ("Line " + (V(selected) + (": " + item V(selected) of "Code")))
  if not((answer==":cancel")):
    CALL backup()
    Code[??12,selected,PZ{E8r-M#W3WR*_R=Yg=] = answer
    status = ??10,Edited
  busy = ??4,0
  dirty = ??4,1

PROC importCode  [warp=false] args=[]
  busy = ??4,1
  ask ??10,Paste code: use | between lines. :cancel keeps current code
  if not((answer==":cancel")):
    CALL loadCode %s(answer)
  busy = ??4,0
  dirty = ??4,1

PROC loadCode %s  [warp=true] args=["source"]
  CALL backup()
  clear(Code)
  ui = ??4,1
  ut = ??10,
  repeat len(arg:"source"):
    uc = letter V(ui) of arg:"source"
    if (V(uc)=="|"):
      append(Code, ??12,ut,Q15ykzA0:WOq37Y/^{L2)
      ut = ??10,
    else:
      if (V(uc)=="\\"):
        ui += ??4,1
        ut = (V(ut) + letter V(ui) of arg:"source")
      else:
        ut = (V(ut) + V(uc))
    ui += ??4,1
  append(Code, ??12,ut,Q15ykzA0:WOq37Y/^{L2)
  selected = ??4,1
  page = ??4,1
  hscroll = ??4,0
  status = ??10,Imported

PROC help  [warp=true] args=[]
  clear(Output)
  CALL hwrap %s("QUICK HELP")
  CALL hwrap %s("Click a line, then type")
  CALL hwrap %s("ENT=new line BK=del TAB=4sp")
  CALL hwrap %s("Arrows: caret / pick a panel")
  CALL hwrap %s("Hover panel + UP/DOWN key")
  CALL hwrap %s("  = scroll that panel 1 line")
  CALL hwrap %s("Vars panel: name = value")
  CALL hwrap %s("Aa button = A/a case switch")
  CALL hwrap %s("IMPORT: | between lines")
  CALL hwrap %s("EXPORT: whole program, 1 line")
  CALL hwrap %s("-- BASICS --")
  CALL hwrap %s("if/elif/else   while")
  CALL hwrap %s("for i in range(1, 9, 2):")
  CALL hwrap %s("for x in [1, 2] / 'text':")
  CALL hwrap %s("break  continue  pass")
  CALL hwrap %s("a, b = b, a   x[i]  x[a:b]")
  CALL hwrap %s("= += -= *= /= %= **= //=")
  CALL hwrap %s("and or not   in  not in  is")
  CALL hwrap %s("-- FUNCTIONS --")
  CALL hwrap %s("def f(a, b=1):  return v")
  CALL hwrap %s("lambda x: x + 1")
  CALL hwrap %s("print input len str int")
  CALL hwrap %s("abs round min max pow chr")
  CALL hwrap %s("-- DATA --")
  CALL hwrap %s("[1, 2]   (3, 4)   {\"a\": 1}")
  CALL hwrap %s("a.append(x) sort pop len sum")
  CALL hwrap %s("s.split join strip replace")
  CALL hwrap %s("f'{x}'    '%s' % ('cat', 4)")
  CALL hwrap %s("-- MORE --")
  CALL hwrap %s("class A:  __init__  self.x")
  CALL hwrap %s("obj.method(args)")
  CALL hwrap %s("try: / except Name: / raise")
  CALL hwrap %s("import math  [x*x for x in r]")
  CALL hwrap %s("yield -> list()  sum()")
  CALL hwrap %s("DEMO button: 13 samples")
  CALL hwrap %s("Double-click line no.: edit")
  CALL hwrap %s("SP button = fast / slow run")
  CALL hwrap %s("STEP: 1 line, inside def too")
  CALL hwrap %s("IMPORT keep | as \\| in code")
  outpage = ??4,1
  dirty = ??4,1

PROC example  [warp=true] args=[]
  CALL backup()
  clear(Code)
  if (V(demo)=="1"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Functions)
    append(Code, ??10,def area(w, h):)
    append(Code, ??10,    return w * h)
    append(Code, ??10,def greet(name, hi='Hello'):)
    append(Code, ??10,    return hi + ', ' + name)
    append(Code, ??10,print(area(3, 4)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(greet('MiniPy')))
    append(Code, ??10,print(greet('Py', 'Yo')))
  if (V(demo)=="2"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Recursion)
    append(Code, ??10,def fact(n):)
    append(Code, ??10,    if n <= 1:)
    append(Code, ??10,        return 1)
    append(Code, ??10,    return n * fact(n - 1))
    append(Code, ??10,def fib(n):)
    append(Code, ??10,    if n < 2:)
    append(Code, ??10,        return n)
    append(Code, ??10,    return fib(n - 1) + fib(n - 2))
    append(Code, ??10,print(fact(5)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(fib(7)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(fact(3) + fib(6)))
  if (V(demo)=="3"):
    append(Code, ??10,# String methods)
    append(Code, ??10,s = '  Hello MiniPy  ')
    append(Code, ??10,t = s.strip())
    append(Code, ??10,print(t.upper()))
    append(Code, ??10,print(t.lower()))
    append(Code, ??10,print(t.replace('MiniPy', 'World')))
    append(Code, ??10,print(t.startswith('Hello')))
    append(Code, ??10,print(t.count('l')))
    append(Code, ??10,print(len(t)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(t[6:12]))
  if (V(demo)=="4"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Lists and loops)
    append(Code, ??10,a = [3, 1, 2])
    append(Code, ??10,a.append(4))
    append(Code, ??10,a.sort())
    append(Code, ??10,print(a))
    append(Code, ??10,print(a[0], a[-1], len(a), sum(a)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(a[1:3]))
    append(Code, ??10,b = [x * x for x in range(6)])
    append(Code, ??10,print(b))
    append(Code, ??10,print([x for x in b if x % 2 == 0]))
  if (V(demo)=="5"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Tuples and unpacking)
    append(Code, ??10,a, b = 1, 2)
    append(Code, ??10,a, b = b, a)
    append(Code, ??10,print(a, b))
    append(Code, ??10,p = (3, 4))
    append(Code, ??10,print(p[0] + p[1]))
    append(Code, ??10,print(divmod(7, 2)))
    append(Code, ??10,r = divmod(9, 4))
    append(Code, ??10,print(r[0], r[1]))
  if (V(demo)=="6"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Dictionaries)
    append(Code, ??10,d = {'a': 1, 'b': 2, 'c': 3})
    append(Code, ??10,print(d['a'] + d['b']))
    append(Code, ??10,print(d['c'] * 2))
    append(Code, ??10,print(d['a'], d['b'], d['c']))
  if (V(demo)=="7"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Exceptions)
    append(Code, ??10,try:)
    append(Code, ??10,    x = 10 / 0)
    append(Code, ??10,except ZeroDivisionError:)
    append(Code, ??10,    print('caught divide by zero'))
    append(Code, ??10,def check(n):)
    append(Code, ??10,    if n < 0:)
    append(Code, ??10,        raise ValueError('negative'))
    append(Code, ??10,    return n * 2)
    append(Code, ??10,try:)
    append(Code, ??10,    print(check(-2)))
    append(Code, ??10,except ValueError:)
    append(Code, ??10,    print('caught raise'))
  if (V(demo)=="8"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Classes)
    append(Code, ??10,class Point:)
    append(Code, ??10,    def __init__(self, x, y):)
    append(Code, ??10,        self.x = x)
    append(Code, ??10,        self.y = y)
    append(Code, ??10,    def norm2(self):)
    append(Code, ??10,        return self.x * self.x + self.y * self.y)
    append(Code, ??10,p = Point(3, 4))
    append(Code, ??10,print(p.norm2()))
    append(Code, ??10,q = Point(1, 1))
    append(Code, ??10,print(q.norm2()))
  if (V(demo)=="9"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Generators)
    append(Code, ??10,def squares(n):)
    append(Code, ??10,    for i in range(n):)
    append(Code, ??10,        yield i * i)
    append(Code, ??10,    return)
    append(Code, ??10,print(list(squares(5))))
    append(Code, ??10,print(sum(squares(4))))
  if (V(demo)=="10"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Modern expressions)
    append(Code, ??10,sq = [x * x for x in range(6) if x % 2 == 0])
    append(Code, ??10,print(sq))
    append(Code, ??10,add = lambda a, b: a + b)
    append(Code, ??10,print(add(2, 3)))
    append(Code, ??10,name = 'Py')
    append(Code, ??10,n = 7)
    append(Code, ??10,print(f'{name} v{n}'))
    append(Code, ??10,print('%s -> %d' % ('cat', 4)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(7 / 2, 7 // 2, 7 % 2, 2 ** 5))
  if (V(demo)=="11"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Math module)
    append(Code, ??10,import math)
    append(Code, ??10,print(math.floor(3.7), math.ceil(3.2)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(math.sqrt(16.0)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(math.pow(2.0, 10.0)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(abs(-4), round(2.5), max(1, 5, 3)))
    append(Code, ??10,print(7 / 2, 1 / 3))
  if (V(demo)=="12"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Bits and bools)
    append(Code, ??10,a = 12)
    append(Code, ??10,# a = 0b1100)
    append(Code, ??10,b = 10)
    append(Code, ??10,# b = 0b1010)
    append(Code, ??10,print(a & b, a | b, a ^ b, a << 1, a >> 2))
    append(Code, ??10,print(~a, 5 // 2))
    append(Code, ??10,print(any([0, 0, 1]), all([1, 2])))
    append(Code, ??10,print(1 in [1, 2], 3 not in [1, 2]))
    append(Code, ??10,if a > b: print('a wins'))
    append(Code, ??10,else: print('b wins'))
  if (V(demo)=="13"):
    append(Code, ??10,# Input and conversion)
    append(Code, ??10,name = input('Your name?'))
    append(Code, ??10,print('Hello, ' + name))
    append(Code, ??10,n = int(input('A number?')))
    append(Code, ??10,print(n * n, n + 1))
  demo += ??4,1
  if (V(demo)>"13"):
    demo = ??4,1
  selected = ??4,1
  page = ??4,1
  hscroll = ??4,0
  status = ("Demo " + (V(demo)-"1"))
  dirty = ??4,1

PROC keepCursor  [warp=true] args=[]
  if ("cursor"<"0"):
    cursor = ??4,0
  if ("cursor">len(item "selected" of "Code")):
    cursor = len(item "selected" of "Code")
  if ("cursor"<"hscroll"):
    hscroll = "cursor"
  if ("cursor">("hscroll"+"49")):
    hscroll = ("cursor"-"49")
  if ("hscroll"<"0"):
    hscroll = ??4,0
  dirty = ??4,1

PROC prepEdit  [warp=true] args=[]
  if ("saved"=="0"):
    CALL backup()
    saved = ??4,1

PROC insert %s  [warp=true] args=["ch"]
  if (("busy"=="0") and ("running"=="0")):
    CALL prepEdit()
    ut = item "selected" of "Code"
    ub = ??10,
    ui = ??4,1
    repeat "cursor":
      ub = ("ub" + letter "ui" of "ut")
      ui += ??4,1
    ub = ("ub" + arg:"ch")
    ui = ("cursor"+"1")
    repeat (len("ut")-"cursor"):
      ub = ("ub" + letter "ui" of "ut")
      ui += ??4,1
    Code["selected"] = "ub"
    cursor += len(arg:"ch")
    status = ??10,Typing
    CALL keepCursor()

PROC newline  [warp=true] args=[]
  if (("busy"=="0") and ("running"=="0")):
    ut = item "selected" of "Code"
    ub = ??10,
    uc = ??10,
    ui = ??4,1
    repeat "cursor":
      ub = ("ub" + letter "ui" of "ut")
      ui += ??4,1
    repeat (len("ut")-"cursor"):
      uc = ("uc" + letter "ui" of "ut")
      ui += ??4,1
    CALL prepEdit()
    Code["selected"] = "ub"
    insert Code[("selected"+"1")] = "uc"
    selected += ??4,1
    cursor = ??4,0
    saved = ??4,0
    status = ??10,New line
    CALL reveal()

PROC backspace  [warp=true] args=[]
  if (("busy"=="0") and ("running"=="0")):
    if ("cursor">"0"):
      CALL prepEdit()
      ut = item "selected" of "Code"
      ub = ??10,
      ui = ??4,1
      repeat ("cursor"-"1"):
        ub = ("ub" + letter "ui" of "ut")
        ui += ??4,1
      ui += ??4,1
      repeat (len("ut")-"cursor"):
        ub = ("ub" + letter "ui" of "ut")
        ui += ??4,1
      Code["selected"] = "ub"
      cursor += ??4,-1
      status = ??10,Backspace
      CALL keepCursor()
    else:
      if ("selected">"1"):
        CALL prepEdit()
        ut = item ("selected"-"1") of "Code"
        cursor = len("ut")
        Code[("selected"-"1")] = ("ut" + item "selected" of "Code")
        del Code["selected"]
        selected += ??4,-1
        saved = ??4,0
        status = ??10,Joined lines
        CALL reveal()

PROC moveCursor %s  [warp=true] args=["dir"]
  if (("busy"=="0") and ("running"=="0")):
    if (arg:"dir"<"0"):
      if ("cursor">"0"):
        cursor += ??4,-1
      else:
        if ("selected">"1"):
          selected += ??4,-1
          cursor = len(item "selected" of "Code")
          CALL reveal()
    else:
      if ("cursor"<len(item "selected" of "Code")):
        cursor += ??4,1
      else:
        if ("selected"<length of "Code"):
          selected += ??4,1
          cursor = ??4,0
          CALL reveal()
    saved = ??4,0
    CALL keepCursor()

PROC tab  [warp=true] args=[]
  CALL insert %s("    ")

WHEN event_whenkeypressed a
  if (V(caps)=="1"):
    CALL insert %s("A")
  else:
    CALL insert %s("a")

WHEN event_whenkeypressed b
  if (V(caps)=="1"):
    CALL insert %s("B")
  else:
    CALL insert %s("b")

WHEN event_whenkeypressed c
  if (V(caps)=="1"):
    CALL insert %s("C")
  else:
    CALL insert %s("c")

WHEN event_whenkeypressed d
  if (V(caps)=="1"):
    CALL insert %s("D")
  else:
    CALL insert %s("d")

WHEN event_whenkeypressed e
  if (V(caps)=="1"):
    CALL insert %s("E")
  else:

PROC boundary  [warp=true] args=[]
  lc = length of "LoopHead"

      CALL popLoop()
    else:
      if (item V(lc) of "LoopKind"=="list"):
        lv = (item V(lc) of "LoopValue"+"1")
        if (V(lv)>item V(lc) of "LoopStop"):
          CALL popLoop()

          LoopValue[??12,lc,;}@MSmt8UWm!EZm{qw,:] = ??12,lv,6j}mFY}R~I+OR~J,Y{S4

            CALL assign %s %s %s(item V(lc) of "LoopName", V(ev), V(evtype))
          pc = (item V(lc) of "LoopHead"+"1")
      else:
        if (item V(lc) of "LoopKind"=="str"):
          lv = (item V(lc) of "LoopValue"+"1")
          if (item V(lc) of "LoopStop"<V(lv)):
            CALL popLoop()
          else:
            LoopValue[??12,lc,;}@MSmt8UWm!EZm{qw,:] = ??12,lv,6j}mFY}R~I+OR~J,Y{S4
            CALL assign %s %s %s(item V(lc) of "LoopName", letter V(lv) of item V(lc) of "LoopText", "S")
